westminster fol ii on the church of st mary matfelon he relates that the murderer of a certain widow was set upon by the wives of the parish who slew him out of hand and for this fact it hath been said that parish purchased that name of mary matfelon strype however thinks it more probable that ma felon is a word of hebrew or syriac extraction signifying as much as que nuper enixa est and so fitly applied to st mary and that the church was founded by some knight that had dwelt in the holy land and dedicated to st mary with the babe in her arms which in those eastern countries was called ma felon bullweed or perhaps bullhead hardhead is one of the popular names for knapweed gerarde
